- **investigation-memory**: 调查场景记忆系统 — 在案件调查过程中，当用户说“先记下来”“先放着”“暂缓、以后再看”“感觉不太对但证据还不够”，或出现探索过但未深入的路径、直觉推测、非正式观察和关键决策理由时，使用本技能静默记录为可查询的考察档案；也用于回答“之前关于 X 有什么记录”。不干扰案件推进，不污染证据链。 ([sxd007/investigation-ontology](https://github.com/sxd007/investigation-ontology/tree/HEAD/plugins/investigation-ontology/skills/investigation-memory) · ★4)
- **investigation-method**: Investigate an observed error, bug or strange behavior to a demonstrated cause, without fixing it: facts, perimeter from the map, isolated hypotheses, a reproduction test, bisection. DO NOT TRIGGER WHEN: assessing a change or artifact for defects (use review-method), or the root cause is already established with evidence.
